<file path="tests/data.test.js">
import { test } from "node:test";
import assert from "node:assert/strict";
import { FOODS } from "../js/data/index.js";
import { ALLERGENS, ALLERGEN_FIRST_FOOD, ALLERGEN_STARTERS, CATEGORIES, CHOKING, IRON_STARTERS, OUTCOMES } from "../js/data/meta.js";
import { GUIDE } from "../js/data/guide.js";
import { ICONS } from "../js/data/icons.js";
import { FOOD_ICONS } from "../js/data/food-icons.js";

const categoryIds = new Set(CATEGORIES.map((c) => c.id));
const allergenIds = new Set(ALLERGENS.map((a) => a.id));

test("food ids are unique and URL-safe", () => {
  const seen = new Set();
  for (const f of FOODS) {
    assert.match(f.id, /^[a-z0-9-]+$/, f.id);
    assert.ok(!seen.has(f.id), `duplicate id ${f.id}`);
    seen.add(f.id);
  }
});

test("every food has a valid category, choking level and allergens", () => {
  for (const f of FOODS) {
    assert.ok(categoryIds.has(f.cat), `${f.id}: category ${f.cat}`);
    assert.ok(f.choking in CHOKING, `${f.id}: choking ${f.choking}`);
    for (const a of f.allergens) assert.ok(allergenIds.has(a), `${f.id}: allergen ${a}`);
    assert.ok(ICONS[f.icon], `${f.id}: icon "${f.icon}" exists`);
  }
});

test("serving text exists for every stage a food is allowed at", () => {
  for (const f of FOODS) {
    if (f.age == null) {
      assert.ok(f.wait, `${f.id}: avoided foods explain why`);
      continue;
    }
    if (f.age > 6) assert.ok(f.wait, `${f.id}: foods that wait explain why`);
    for (const [stage, from] of [["6", 6], ["9", 9], ["12", 12]]) {
      const next = stage === "6" ? 9 : stage === "9" ? 12 : Infinity;
      if (f.age < next && f.age <= 12) assert.ok(f.serve[stage], `${f.id}: serve text for ${stage}+ (from ${from})`);
    }
  }
});

test("safety essentials are encoded", () => {
  const byId = Object.fromEntries(FOODS.map((f) => [f.id, f]));
  assert.equal(byId.honey.age, 12);
  assert.equal(byId["cows-milk"].age, 12);
  assert.equal(byId.juice.age, 12);
  assert.equal(byId.popcorn.age, 48);
  assert.equal(byId.swordfish.age, null);
  for (const id of ["grape", "blueberry", "apple", "carrot", "peanut", "hot-dog", "popcorn"]) {
    assert.equal(byId[id].choking, "high", `${id} is a common choking hazard`);
  }
  assert.deepEqual(byId.eggs.allergens, ["egg"]);
  assert.deepEqual(byId.peanut.allergens, ["peanut"]);
});

test("every allergen has at least one food that can be offered at 6 months", () => {
  for (const a of ALLERGENS) {
    assert.ok(FOODS.some((f) => f.allergens.includes(a.id) && f.age === 6), a.id);
  }
});

test("suggested first foods carry their allergen, and iron starters are iron-rich", () => {
  const byId = new Map(FOODS.map((f) => [f.id, f]));
  for (const a of ALLERGENS) {
    const food = byId.get(ALLERGEN_FIRST_FOOD[a.id]);
    assert.ok(food && food.allergens.includes(a.id) && food.age === 6, a.id);
  }
  for (const id of IRON_STARTERS) assert.ok(byId.get(id)?.iron && byId.get(id).age === 6, id);
  for (const c of CATEGORIES) assert.ok(c.short, c.id);
});

test("every outcome has a face icon", () => {
  for (const o of OUTCOMES) assert.ok(ICONS[o.icon], o.id);
});

test("every allergen has first-food ideas", () => {
  for (const a of ALLERGENS) assert.ok(ALLERGEN_STARTERS[a.id], a.id);
  assert.deepEqual(Object.keys(ALLERGEN_STARTERS).sort(), ALLERGENS.map((a) => a.id).sort());
});

test("every food has its own icon mapping, and no mapping is stale", () => {
  const ids = new Set(FOODS.map((f) => f.id));
  for (const f of FOODS) assert.ok(FOOD_ICONS[f.id], `${f.id} has an entry in food-icons.js`);
  for (const id of Object.keys(FOOD_ICONS)) assert.ok(ids.has(id), `food-icons.js entry ${id} matches a food`);
});

test("Solid Starts links are slugs, not URLs", () => {
  for (const f of FOODS) if (f.ss) assert.match(f.ss, /^[a-z0-9-]+$/, f.id);
});

test("guide sections have content", () => {
  for (const s of GUIDE) {
    assert.ok(s.id && s.title && s.intro, s.id);
  }
});

test("sync config is either off (null) or a Firebase settings object", async () => {
  // A syntax error here would stop the published app from syncing, so catch
  // it before deploying.
  const { FIREBASE_CONFIG } = await import("../js/sync-config.js");
  if (FIREBASE_CONFIG === null) return;
  assert.equal(typeof FIREBASE_CONFIG, "object");
  for (const key of ["apiKey", "authDomain", "projectId", "appId"]) {
    assert.equal(typeof FIREBASE_CONFIG[key], "string", `FIREBASE_CONFIG.${key}`);
    assert.ok(FIREBASE_CONFIG[key].length > 0, `FIREBASE_CONFIG.${key} is filled in`);
  }
});

<file path="README.md">
# First Bites

A starting-solids reference and tracker for two parents. Look up how to serve
a food at your baby's age, then log each try: when, how it went, how it was
served, and any reaction.

## What it does

- **366 foods** in 11 categories, each with an icon and:
  - the earliest age to offer it, or why to wait or avoid it
  - how to serve it at 6–8 months, 9–11 months and 12 months +
  - common allergens, choking risk, and whether it's iron-rich
  - safety and nutrition tips
  - a link to the food's page on [Solid Starts](https://solidstarts.com/foods/) for their full guide, photos and videos (356 foods have one)
- **Tracker.** Tap the circle on any food tile and pick a face (loved it → wouldn't try) to log today's try in one tap, with **Undo** if you tap the wrong one. **Add details** covers the day (Today, Yesterday or another day), how it was served (with quick picks like *mashed* or *pre-loaded spoon*), any reaction signs and notes. Each tile shows its status (liked, still learning or reaction, each with its own symbol) and how many times it's been tried.
- **Your own foods.** Add anything that isn't listed, by tapping **Add a food** (or **Add "…"** when a search finds nothing). File it under any category, e.g. kangaroo under Meat & poultry, or under the new **Meals & mixed foods** for pouches and dishes. Say what's in it by picking listed foods: logging the meal then counts as a try of each one (and of its allergens), and their age, choking and allergen advice carries over. A reaction to a meal stays with the meal, with a note on each food in it. Tick any other allergens on the label, and keep the printed ingredients and notes. With sync on, both phones see your foods. Optionally, **read a label from a photo** to fill the form in (see below).
- **What's next.** Before 6 months, a countdown and a readiness checklist you both can tick. After that, ideas for what to offer next: allergens due again, the next allergen to introduce, your to-try list and iron-rich foods.
- **Progress.** A ring for foods tried against your goal (100 before the first birthday by default), the nine common allergens as a grid (with a nudge when one hasn't been offered in a week), a to-try list, progress by category, and recent tries grouped by day, showing who logged each one.
- **Guide.** Sections that fold open: readiness signs (as a shared checklist), safe set-up, gagging vs. choking, sizes and shapes by age, common choking hazards, allergen introduction, reaction signs, foods to avoid, iron, and how much to offer.
- **Search and filters** (search stays at the top as you scroll, with a bar to jump between categories; the rest is behind the Filters button) by name (including other names like *courgette* and *capsicum*), category, allergen, what's safe at your baby's age, iron-rich, choking hazards, and tried/liked/still learning/reaction/to-try.

## About the food information

The food notes in `js/data/` were written for this app. They are general
guidance based on widely published advice from pediatric and public-health
bodies (AAP, NHS, FDA and others). The app does not copy Solid Starts' content:
it's their copyrighted work, their terms of use don't allow automated
collection of it, and this repository is public. Each food links to the
matching Solid Starts page instead. `npm run check:links` checks those links
against the sitemap Solid Starts publishes for search engines.

This is not medical advice. Talk to your pediatrician about readiness, allergy
risk (especially with severe eczema or an existing food allergy) and any concerns.

## Using it

There are three ways to run it. All use the same code.

| | Who can open it | Do both phones share one log? | Setup |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| GitHub Pages + Firebase sync | Anyone with the link can open the app; only your two accounts can see or change the log | Yes, live, works offline | About 15 minutes, free |
| GitHub Pages on its own | Anyone with the link | No, each phone has its own log (swap backups to merge) | About 5 minutes |
| claude.ai Artifact | Only people you share it with | Yes, live | Both of you need claude.ai accounts |

### Publish on GitHub Pages

1. In the repository, go to **Settings → Pages → Build and deployment** and set **Source** to **GitHub Actions**.
2. The workflow in `.github/workflows/pages.yml` runs the tests and publishes the site to `https://<your-username>.github.io/solid-foods-app/` on every push to the repository's default branch. You can also run it by hand from the **Actions** tab (**Test and deploy → Run workflow**).
3. Open that address on each phone and add it to the home screen. On Android, tap **Install** on the banner in the app (or **Install First Bites** on the Baby tab); on iPhone, use Safari → Share → **Add to Home Screen**. It works offline.

**About privacy:** on GitHub's Free and Pro plans, a GitHub Pages site is always public. Only GitHub Enterprise can limit who opens it. The app asks search engines not to list it, but anyone with the link can open the food guide. Your baby's log is never part of the site: it stays on each phone, or, with sync set up, in your own Firebase database, which only your two accounts can read.

### Syncing between your phones (Firebase)

Firebase is Google's app database service. The free plan is plenty for this app.

1. **Create a project.** Go to <https://console.firebase.google.com>, click **Create a project**, name it (e.g. `first-bites`), and turn off Google Analytics (not needed).
2. **Add a database.** Under **Build → Firestore Database**, click **Create database**, pick a location near you, and start in **production mode**.
3. **Lock it to the two of you.** Open the **Rules** tab, paste the contents of [`firestore.rules`](firestore.rules), replace the two example email addresses with yours and your partner's, and click **Publish**.
4. **Create your logins.** Under **Build → Authentication**, click **Get started**, enable **Email/Password**, then on the **Users** tab click **Add user** once for each of you. To stop anyone else making an account, go to **Settings → User actions** and untick **Enable create (sign-up)**.
5. **Allow your site.** In **Authentication → Settings → Authorized domains**, add `<your-username>.github.io`.
6. **Connect the app.** In **Project settings → General → Your apps**, click the web icon (`</>`), register an app (no hosting needed), and copy the `firebaseConfig` object. Paste it into [`js/sync-config.js`](js/sync-config.js) in place of `null`, for example:

   ```js
   export const FIREBASE_CONFIG = {
     apiKey: "AIza…",
     authDomain: "first-bites-1234.firebaseapp.com",
     projectId: "first-bites-1234",
     storageBucket: "first-bites-1234.appspot.com",
     messagingSenderId: "1234567890",
     appId: "1:1234567890:web:abc123",
   };
   ```

   This config isn't a secret (it only names your project), so it's fine in a public repository. The rules from step 3 are what keep the data private.
7. Commit the change to the default branch. Once the site redeploys, open the **Baby** tab on each phone and sign in. If a phone already has tries saved from before, tap **Move them to the shared log**.

After that, a try logged on either phone shows up on the other within seconds. Logging works offline too, and catches up when the phone reconnects.

### Reading food labels from a photo (optional)

With sync set up, the **Add a food** form can read a label from a photo: take a picture of the ingredients and allergen statement, and the name, brand, ingredients and allergens are filled in for you to check. It uses Google's Gemini through your own Firebase project, on the free plan (no billing account). It needs a one-time setup, about 10 minutes:

1. **Switch on AI Logic.** In the [Firebase console](https://console.firebase.google.com), open your project, go to **AI Services → AI Logic** and click **Get started**. When asked for a Gemini API provider, choose **Gemini Developer API** (the no-cost option) and finish the steps. This also switches on **App Check** for AI Logic, which only lets requests from your site through.
2. **Get a reCAPTCHA key** (App Check uses it to recognise your site). Go to the [reCAPTCHA admin console](https://www.google.com/recaptcha/admin/create), sign in with the same Google account, and create a key: label it *First Bites*, choose **Score based (v3)**, and add the domain `<your-username>.github.io`. Copy the **site key** and the **secret key**.
3. **Register your site with App Check.** In the Firebase console, go to **Security → App Check → Apps**, find your web app, choose **reCAPTCHA**, paste the **secret key** and click **Save**.
4. **Tell the app the site key.** In [`js/sync-config.js`](js/sync-config.js), paste the **site key** between the quotes in `LABEL_READING`, and commit. (The site key isn't a secret; never paste the secret key here.)

Once the site has redeployed, open **Add a food** and tap **Read a label from a photo**.

Good to know:

- Always check what was read against the pack before saving. Allergens come from the label's "Contains" statement and a second check of the printed ingredients, but a photo can be misread.
- The free plan has daily limits, which are plenty for a few labels a day. If you hit one, the app says so; type the food in instead.
- The photo is sent to Google to be read and isn't kept by the app. On the free plan, Google may use what's sent to improve its products.
- If the reCAPTCHA site sends you to Google Cloud to create the key instead, create a score-based **Website** key for your domain there, register it in App Check as **reCAPTCHA Enterprise**, and add `provider: "recaptcha-enterprise"` next to the site key in `js/sync-config.js`.

### claude.ai Artifact

`npm run build:artifact` builds `dist/first-bites.html`, a single page that can
be published as a claude.ai Artifact. There the tracker uses the artifact's
own shared database: invite your partner from the artifact's Share menu as an
**Editor**.

### Run it locally

```sh
npm start        # serves the app at http://localhost:8080
npm test         # data and logic tests (Node 20+)
```

The app itself needs no build step: it is plain HTML, CSS and ES modules.

## Project layout

| Path | What's there |
| --- | --- |
| `index.html`, `css/styles.css` | Page shell and styles (light and dark) |
| `js/app.js` | Screens, routing and event handling |
| `js/logic.js` | Pure functions: ages, statuses, allergen progress, backup merge |
| `js/store.js` | Storage: this device, the claude.ai shared database, or Firebase |
| `js/cloud.js`, `js/sync-config.js` | Optional Firebase sync and label reading: loader and your project config |
| `js/label.js` | Reading a label from a photo: the request to the model and matching its answer to the food list |
| `js/data/*.js` | The food list (one file per category), guide text and icon map |
| `js/data/icons.js` | Generated food icons (`npm run build:icons`) |
| `js/vendor/` | Generated Firebase bundles (`npm run build:firebase`): `firebase.js` for syncing, `firebase-ai.js` for label reading, and the core they share |
| `sw.js`, `manifest.webmanifest`, `icons/` | Offline support and home-screen install |
| `firestore.rules` | Security rules template for Firebase |
| `scripts/` | Build scripts for the icons, Firebase bundle and Artifact page, and the link checker |
| `tests/` | `node --test` suites |

### Adding or editing a food

Each entry in `js/data/<category>.js` is a call to `food()`:

```js
food("avocado", "Avocado", "fruit", {
  aka: ["alligator pear"],           // other names for search
  age: 6,                            // earliest age in months (null = avoid)
  wait: "",                          // why to wait, when age > 6 or null
  allergens: [],                     // milk, egg, peanut, treenut, sesame, soy, wheat, fish, shellfish
  choking: "low",                    // low | prep | high
  iron: false,
  s6: "…", s9: "…", s12: "…",        // how to serve at each stage
  tips: ["…"],
  ss: "avocado",                     // Solid Starts slug (defaults to the id; null for no link)
})
```

Then give it an icon in `js/data/food-icons.js`. Use any name from the [Fluent Emoji Flat set](https://icon-sets.iconify.design/fluent-emoji-flat/), or define a recolored variant in `scripts/build-icons.mjs`, and run `npm run build:icons`.

Keep ids stable: logged tries reference them. Run `npm test` after editing to check the data.

Third-party licenses (food icons, Firebase) are in [`THIRD_PARTY_NOTICES.md`](THIRD_PARTY_NOTICES.md).
</file>

<file path="sw.js">
// Offline support for the installed app.
//
// The app's own files are fetched from the network first, so a newly
// published version shows up on the next load; the cached copy is only used
// when offline. Google Fonts come from the cache once downloaded. Everything
// else (Firebase sync traffic, links out) is left alone: caching those would
// serve stale data.
const CACHE = "first-bites-v8";
const SHELL = [
  "./",
  "index.html",
  "css/styles.css",
  "manifest.webmanifest",
  "icons/icon.svg",
  "icons/icon-192.png",
  "js/app.js",
  "js/logic.js",
  "js/store.js",
  "js/cloud.js",
  "js/label.js",
  "js/sync-config.js",
  "js/vendor/firebase.js",
  "js/vendor/chunk-UQGNWA5L.js",
  "js/data/index.js",
  "js/data/icons.js",
  "js/data/food-icons.js",
  "js/data/define.js",
  "js/data/meta.js",
  "js/data/guide.js",
  "js/data/fruits.js",
  "js/data/vegetables.js",
  "js/data/grains.js",
  "js/data/legumes.js",
  "js/data/meat.js",
  "js/data/fish.js",
  "js/data/dairy.js",
  "js/data/nuts.js",
  "js/data/herbs.js",
  "js/data/pantry.js",
  "js/data/drinks.js",
];

const FONT_HOSTS = new Set(["fonts.googleapis.com", "fonts.gstatic.com"]);

self.addEventListener("install", (event) => {
  // cache: "reload" skips the browser's HTTP cache, so the precache holds
  // what is on the server now, not a copy from a few minutes ago.
  event.waitUntil(
    caches
      .open(CACHE)
      .then((cache) => cache.addAll(SHELL.map((url) => new Request(url, { cache: "reload" }))))
      .then(() => self.skipWaiting()),
  );
});

self.addEventListener("activate", (event) => {
  event.waitUntil(
    caches
      .keys()
      .then((keys) => Promise.all(keys.filter((k) => k !== CACHE).map((k) => caches.delete(k))))
      .then(() => self.clients.claim()),
  );
});

self.addEventListener("fetch", (event) => {
  const { request } = event;
  if (request.method !== "GET") return;
  const url = new URL(request.url);
  if (url.origin === self.location.origin) event.respondWith(networkFirst(request));
  else if (FONT_HOSTS.has(url.hostname)) event.respondWith(cacheFirst(request));
});

async function networkFirst(request) {
  const cache = await caches.open(CACHE);
  try {
    // "no-cache" asks the server whether the file changed (cheap when it hasn't).
    const response = await fetch(request, { cache: "no-cache" });
    if (response.ok) cache.put(request, response.clone());
    return response;
  } catch (err) {
    const cached = (await cache.match(request)) || (request.mode === "navigate" && (await cache.match("./")));
    if (cached) return cached;
    throw err;
  }
}

async function cacheFirst(request) {
  const cache = await caches.open(CACHE);
  const cached = await cache.match(request);
  if (cached) return cached;
  const response = await fetch(request);
  if (response.ok || response.type === "opaque") cache.put(request, response.clone());
  return response;
}
</file>

const OUTCOME_LABEL = Object.fromEntries(OUTCOMES.map((o) => [o.id, o.label]));

const VIEWS = ["foods", "progress", "guide", "setup"];

  store: null,

  summaries: new Map(),
  view: "foods",
  names: {},
  filters: { q: "", status: "all", cat: "all", allergen: "all", flag: "all", sort: "category" },

// The built-in foods plus the family's own, rebuilt whenever the log
// changes. Everything that lists or looks up foods goes through these.
let foodList = FOODS;
let foodMap = FOODS_BY_ID;

function rebuildFoods() {
  const own = Object.values(app.state.foods || {}).map((f) => L.expandCustomFood(f, FOODS_BY_ID, CATEGORY_ICONS));
  foodList = own.length ? [...own, ...FOODS].sort((a, b) => a.name.localeCompare(b.name)) : FOODS;
  foodMap = new Map(foodList.map((f) => [f.id, f]));
}

function foodById(id) {
  return foodMap.get(id);
}

// Foods tried toward the goal: a mix counts as what's in it.
function triedFoods() {
  const out = new Map();
  for (const [id, s] of app.summaries) if (L.countsAsFood(foodById(id))) out.set(id, s);
  return out;
}
const main = $("#view");

// ---------- helpers ----------

function esc(value) {
  return String(value ?? "").replace(/[&<>"']/g, (c) => ({ "&": "&amp;", "<": "&lt;", ">": "&gt;", '"': "&quot;", "'": "&#39;" })[c]);
}

const dateFmt = new Intl.DateTimeFormat(undefined, { day: "numeric", month: "short" });
const dateFmtYear = new Intl.DateTimeFormat(undefined, { day: "numeric", month: "short", year: "numeric" });

function fmtDate(iso) {
  if (!iso) return "";
  const [y, m, d] = iso.split("-").map(Number);
  const date = new Date(y, m - 1, d);
  return (y === new Date().getFullYear() ? dateFmt : dateFmtYear).format(date);
}

function relDays(n) {
  if (n == null) return "";
  if (n <= 0) return "today";
  if (n === 1) return "yesterday";
  return `${n} days ago`;
}

function plural(n, word, many = `${word}s`) {
  return `${n} ${n === 1 ? word : many}`;
}

function babyMonths() {
  return L.ageInMonths(app.state.profile.birthDate);
}

function babyName() {
  return app.state.profile.babyName || "";
}

function goal() {
  return app.state.profile.goal || DEFAULT_GOAL;
}

function canWrite() {
  return !!app.store && app.store.canWrite;
}

function loading() {
  return !app.store || (app.store.mode !== "local" && !app.store.ready && !app.store.error);
}

// Why the shared log couldn't be read, or "" if it's fine.
function loadError() {
  const err = app.store && app.store.error;
  if (!err) return "";
  if (["permission-denied", "unauthenticated", "revoked", "not_granted"].includes(err.code)) return describeStoreError(err);
  return "Check your connection, then try again.";
}

// Sync is set up for this app (or meant to be), but this phone is using its
// own log.
function phoneOnly() {
  return !!(app.syncConfig || app.syncConfigError) && !!app.store && app.store.mode === "local";
}

// Tries saved on this phone while signed out, not yet in the shared log.
function unsyncedLocalIds() {
  if (!app.store || app.store.mode !== "cloud" || !app.store.ready) return [];
  return Object.keys(readLocalState(KNOWN_IDS).logs).filter((id) => !app.state.logs[id]);
}

function ageTag(food) {
  if (food.age == null) return "Avoid";
  if (food.age >= 24) return `${food.age / 12}y`;
  return `${food.age}m`;
}

function isWaiting(food) {
  return !L.isReadyAt(food, babyMonths());
}

// One hidden sprite holds every icon; tiles reference it with <use>.
function mountIconSprite() {
  if (document.getElementById("icon-sprite")) return;
  const symbols = Object.entries(ICONS)
    .map(([key, body]) => `<symbol id="ic-${key}" viewBox="${ICON_VIEWBOX}">${body}</symbol>`)
    .join("");
  const holder = document.createElement("div");
  holder.innerHTML = `<svg id="icon-sprite" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false" style="position:absolute;width:0;height:0;overflow:hidden">${symbols}</svg>`;
  document.body.prepend(holder.firstChild);
}

function iconHTML(food, cls = "food-icon") {
  return `<svg class="${cls}" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use href="#ic-${food.icon}"></use></svg>`;
}

function whoLogged(log) {
  if (log.by && app.names[log.by]) return app.names[log.by];
  return log.byName || "";
}

// Shown as a popover where supported, so it sits above an open food page or
// form. It moves into the topmost open dialog, because everything outside a
// modal dialog is inert and its Undo button couldn't be tapped there.
let toastTimer;
let toastUndo = null;
const toastEl = $("#toast");
const canPopover = typeof toastEl.showPopover === "function";

function showToastIn(host) {
  if (canPopover && toastEl.matches(":popover-open")) toastEl.hidePopover();
  if (toastEl.parentElement !== host) host.append(toastEl);
  toastEl.hidden = false;
  if (canPopover) toastEl.showPopover();
}

// Replace a dialog's content without losing a message shown inside it.
function setDialogHTML(dialog, html) {
  const keep = dialog.contains(toastEl) && !toastEl.hidden;
  dialog.innerHTML = html;
  if (keep) showToastIn(dialog);
}

function toastHost() {
  const open = document.querySelectorAll("dialog[open]");
  return open.length ? open[open.length - 1] : document.body;
}

function toast(message, { undo } = {}) {
  toastUndo = undo || null;
  toastEl.innerHTML = `<span>${esc(message)}</span>${undo ? '<button type="button" class="toast-action" data-action="undo">Undo</button>' : ""}`;
  showToastIn(toastHost());
  clearTimeout(toastTimer);
  toastTimer = setTimeout(hideToast, undo ? 6000 : 3200);
}

function hideToast() {
  if (canPopover && toastEl.matches(":popover-open")) toastEl.hidePopover();
  toastEl.hidden = true;
  toastUndo = null;
}

async function undoLast() {
  const undo = toastUndo;
  hideToast();
  if (!undo) return;
  try {
    await undo();
    toast("Undone");
  } catch (err) {
    toast(describeStoreError(err));
  }
}

// ---------- chrome ----------

function renderChrome() {
  for (const tab of document.querySelectorAll(".tab")) {
    if (tab.dataset.view === app.view) tab.setAttribute("aria-current", "page");
    else tab.removeAttribute("aria-current");
  }
  const chip = $("#baby-chip");
  const name = babyName();
  const months = babyMonths();
  const tried = triedFoods().size;
  if (name || months != null) {
    chip.innerHTML = `<strong>${esc(name || "Baby")}</strong>${months != null ? `<span class="num muted">· ${esc(L.formatAge(months))}</span>` : ""}`;
    chip.setAttribute("aria-label", `${name || "Baby"}: ${months != null ? L.formatAge(months) + ", " : ""}${tried} foods tried. Edit baby details.`);
  } else {
    chip.innerHTML = "<strong>Add baby</strong>";
    chip.setAttribute("aria-label", "Add your baby's details");
  }
}

// ---------- foods view ----------

const STATUS_FILTERS = [
  { id: "all", label: "All" },
  { id: "new", label: "Not tried" },
  { id: "tried", label: "Tried" },
  { id: "liked", label: "Liked" },
  { id: "learning", label: "Still learning" },
  { id: "reaction", label: "Reaction" },
  { id: "queued", label: "To try next" },
];

function statusOf(food) {
  const s = app.summaries.get(food.id);
  return s ? s.status : "new";
}

function matchesStatus(food, status) {
  const st = statusOf(food);
  switch (status) {
    case "new":
      return st === "new";
    case "tried":
      return st !== "new";
    case "queued":
      return !!app.state.queue[food.id];
    case "all":
      return true;
    default:
      return st === status;
  }
}

function matchesFilters(food, { skipStatus = false } = {}) {
  const f = app.filters;
  if (!skipStatus && !matchesStatus(food, f.status)) return false;
  if (f.cat !== "all" && food.cat !== f.cat) return false;
  if (f.allergen === "any" && !food.allergens.length) return false;
  if (f.allergen !== "all" && f.allergen !== "any" && !food.allergens.includes(f.allergen)) return false;
  if (f.flag === "ready" && isWaiting(food)) return false;
  if (f.flag === "wait" && !isWaiting(food)) return false;
  if (f.flag === "iron" && !food.iron) return false;
  if (f.flag === "hazard" && food.choking !== "high") return false;
  return L.matchesQuery(food, f.q);
}

function readyFilterLabel() {
  const months = babyMonths();
  return months != null && months >= 6 ? `Ready at ${L.formatAge(months)}` : "Ready from 6 months";
}

// Problems with the log come first. Ask for a birth date only once we know
// there isn't one: synced details can arrive a moment after the page draws.
function statusNoticesHTML() {
  const notices = [];
  const error = loadError();
  if (error) {
    notices.push(`<div class="notice notice-bad" role="alert"><p><strong>Your shared log didn't load.</strong> ${esc(error)}</p><button type="button" class="btn btn-small" data-action="retry-store">Try again</button></div>`);
  } else if (phoneOnly() && app.syncConfigError) {
    notices.push(`<div class="notice notice-bad"><p><strong>Syncing is off.</strong> The sync settings have a problem, so tries are saved on this phone only.</p><a class="btn btn-small" href="#setup">Details</a></div>`);
  } else if (phoneOnly() && app.cloud.status === "error") {
    notices.push(`<div class="notice notice-bad"><p><strong>Sync isn't connected.</strong> ${esc(app.cloud.message)} Tries are saved on this phone until it reconnects.</p><button type="button" class="btn btn-small" data-action="retry-cloud">Try again</button></div>`);
  } else if (phoneOnly() && app.cloud.status === "signed-out") {
    notices.push(`<div class="notice"><p><strong>You're signed out.</strong> Sign in to see your shared log. Until then, tries are saved on this phone only.</p><a class="btn btn-small btn-primary" href="#setup">Sign in</a></div>`);
  }
  const unsynced = unsyncedLocalIds().length;
  if (unsynced) {
    notices.push(`<div class="notice"><p>This phone has ${plural(unsynced, "try", "tries")} logged while you were signed out.</p><button type="button" class="btn btn-small btn-primary" data-action="copy-local">Move to shared log</button></div>`);
  }
  const settling = loading() || app.cloud.status === "loading";
  if (!notices.length && !settling && !phoneOnly() && !app.state.profile.birthDate) {
    notices.push(`<div class="notice"><p>Add your baby's birth date to see the right serving stage for their age.</p><a class="btn btn-small" href="#setup">Add baby details</a></div>`);
  }
  return notices.join("");
}

// The search bar and category jump bar stay put while the list scrolls.

// ---------- what's next ----------

// Before solids: a countdown to 6 months and the readiness signs to tick.
// After: a few ideas for what to offer next.
function nextCardHTML() {
  const months = babyMonths();
  if (months == null || loading() || loadError()) return "";
  if (months < 6 && !app.summaries.size) return startingSoonHTML(months);
  const ideas = L.suggestNext({ foods: foodList, summaries: app.summaries, queue: app.state.queue, months, firstFoods: ALLERGEN_FIRST_FOOD, ironStarters: IRON_STARTERS });
  if (!ideas.length) return "";
  const reason = (idea) => {
    if (idea.kind === "due") return `${ALLERGEN_LABEL[idea.allergen]} last offered ${relDays(idea.daysSince)}. Offer it again to keep it in the diet.`;
    if (idea.kind === "allergen") return `First taste of ${ALLERGEN_LABEL[idea.allergen].toLowerCase()}: ${ALLERGEN_STARTERS[idea.allergen]}.`;
    if (idea.kind === "queued") return "On your to-try list.";
    return "Iron-rich. Babies need extra iron from about 6 months.";
  };
  return `<section class="next-card" aria-labelledby="next-title">
    <h2 id="next-title">Ideas for next</h2>
    <ul class="next-list">${ideas
      .map((idea) => {
        const food = foodById(idea.id);
        return `<li><button type="button" class="next-item" data-open="${food.id}">
          <span class="mini-tile" data-cat="${food.cat}" aria-hidden="true">${iconHTML(food)}</span>
          <span><strong>${esc(food.name)}</strong><span class="sub">${esc(reason(idea))}</span></span>
        </button></li>`;
      })
      .join("")}</ul>
  </section>`;
}

function readySignsHTML({ details = false } = {}) {
  const ticked = new Set(app.state.profile.readySigns || []);
  const signs = GUIDE.find((g) => g.id === "ready").items;
  return `<ul class="ready-list">${signs
    .map((item) => `<li><label class="check"><input type="checkbox" data-ready-sign="${item.id}"${ticked.has(item.id) ? " checked" : ""}${canWrite() ? "" : " disabled"}><span><strong>${esc(item.title)}</strong>${details ? `<span class="sub">${esc(item.text)}</span>` : ""}</span></label></li>`)
    .join("")}</ul>`;
}

function startingSoonHTML(months) {
  const birth = app.state.profile.birthDate;
  const [y, m, d] = birth.split("-").map(Number);
  const sixMonths = L.toISODate(new Date(y, m - 1 + 6, d));
  const days = L.daysBetween(L.todayISO(), sixMonths);
  const when = days <= 0 ? "now" : days < 63 ? `in about ${plural(Math.max(1, Math.round(days / 7)), "week")}` : `in about ${plural(Math.round(days / 30.4), "month")}`;
  const ticked = (app.state.profile.readySigns || []).length;
  const total = GUIDE.find((g) => g.id === "ready").items.length;
  return `<section class="next-card" aria-labelledby="next-title">
    <h2 id="next-title">Getting ready for solids</h2>
    <p>${esc(babyName() || "Your baby")} is ${esc(L.formatAgeLong(months))} old. Most babies are ready at about 6 months: <strong>${esc(when)}</strong> (${esc(fmtDate(sixMonths))}).</p>
    <p class="muted">Look for all four signs together. <span class="num" data-ready-count>${ticked} of ${total}</span> ticked so far.</p>
    ${readySignsHTML()}
    <a class="btn btn-small" href="#guide">Read the guide together</a>
  </section>`;
}

async function toggleReadySign(input) {
  const signs = new Set(app.state.profile.readySigns || []);
  if (input.checked) signs.add(input.dataset.readySign);
  else signs.delete(input.dataset.readySign);
  try {
    await app.store.setProfile({ readySigns: [...signs] });
  } catch (err) {
    input.checked = !input.checked;
    toast(describeStoreError(err));
  }
}

// ---------- scrolling helpers ----------

// Highlight the category in view, show Back to top, and shade the sticky bars
// once the list is scrolling under them.
function updateScrollUI() {
  const toTop = $("#to-top");
  if (!toTop) return;
  toTop.hidden = scrollY < innerHeight * 1.5;
  const bar = $("#search-bar");
  const nav = $("#cat-jump");
  const barBottom = bar.getBoundingClientRect().bottom;
  const navRect = nav.getBoundingClientRect();
  const navStuck = !nav.hidden && $("#food-results").getBoundingClientRect().top < navRect.bottom - 1;
  bar.classList.toggle("is-stuck", !navStuck && $(".filters", main).getBoundingClientRect().top < barBottom - 1);
  nav.classList.toggle("is-stuck", navStuck);
  if (nav.hidden) return;
  const line = navRect.bottom + 16;
  let current = null;
  for (const group of document.querySelectorAll(".group")) {
    if (group.getBoundingClientRect().top > line) break;
    current = group.dataset.cat;
  }
  current ||= document.querySelector(".group")?.dataset.cat;
  for (const chip of nav.querySelectorAll(".chip")) {
    const on = chip.dataset.group === current;
    if (on && chip.getAttribute("aria-current") !== "true") {
      // Leave room for the edge fade, so the current chip shows clearly.
      const row = $("#cat-chips");
      row.scrollTo({ left: chip.offsetLeft - row.offsetLeft - 56, behavior: smooth() });
    }
    if (on) chip.setAttribute("aria-current", "true");
    else chip.removeAttribute("aria-current");
  }
}

let scrollQueued = false;
window.addEventListener(
  "scroll",
  () => {
    if (scrollQueued || app.view !== "foods") return;
    scrollQueued = true;
    requestAnimationFrame(() => {
      scrollQueued = false;
      updateScrollUI();
    });
  },
  { passive: true },
);

function renderCatJump(groups) {
  const nav = $("#cat-jump");
  nav.hidden = groups.length < 2;
  $("#cat-chips").innerHTML = groups.map((g) => `<button type="button" class="chip" data-group="${g.id}">${esc(g.short)}</button>`).join("");
  updateChipFade($("#cat-chips"));
}

function smooth() {
  return matchMedia("(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)").matches ? "auto" : "smooth";
}

function renderStatusChips() {
  const row = $("#status-chips");
  if (!row) return;
  const base = foodList.filter((food) => matchesFilters(food, { skipStatus: true }));
  row.innerHTML = STATUS_FILTERS.map((s) => {
    const n = base.filter((food) => matchesStatus(food, s.id)).length;
    return `<button type="button" class="chip" data-status="${s.id}" aria-pressed="${app.filters.status === s.id}">${esc(s.label)} <span class="count">${n}</span></button>`;
  }).join("");
  updateChipFade(row);
}

// Fade whichever edge of the status row has more chips beyond it.
function updateChipFade(row) {
  const max = row.scrollWidth - row.clientWidth;
  row.classList.toggle("fade-start", row.scrollLeft > 2);
  row.classList.toggle("fade-end", row.scrollLeft < max - 2);
}

// ---------- filter sheet ----------

const FILTER_KEYS = ["cat", "allergen", "flag"];

function filterGroups() {
  return [
    { key: "cat", legend: "Category", options: [["all", "All"], ...CATEGORIES.map((c) => [c.id, c.label])] },
    { key: "allergen", legend: "Contains", options: [["all", "Any food"], ["any", "Any common allergen"], ...ALLERGENS.map((a) => [a.id, a.label])] },
    { key: "flag", legend: "Age and safety", options: [["all", "All ages"], ["ready", readyFilterLabel()], ["wait", "Not yet / avoid"], ["iron", "Iron-rich"], ["hazard", "Common choking hazards"]] },
    { key: "sort", legend: "Order", options: [["category", "By category"], ["az", "A–Z"], ["recent", "Recently tried"]] },
  ];
}

// How an active filter reads on its removable chip.
function filterLabel(key) {
  const value = app.filters[key];
  if (key === "allergen") return value === "any" ? "Any allergen" : `Contains ${ALLERGEN_LABEL[value].toLowerCase()}`;
  const group = filterGroups().find((g) => g.key === key);
  return group.options.find(([v]) => v === value)[1];
}

function renderFilterBar() {
  const active = FILTER_KEYS.filter((k) => app.filters[k] !== "all");
  const count = $("#filter-count");
  if (!count) return;
  count.textContent = active.length;
  count.hidden = !active.length;
  $("#filter-btn").setAttribute("aria-label", active.length ? `Filters (${active.length} on)` : "Filters");
  const box = $("#active-filters");
  box.hidden = !active.length;
  box.innerHTML = active
    .map((k) => `<button type="button" class="chip chip-on" data-action="clear-filter" data-key="${k}" aria-label="Remove filter: ${esc(filterLabel(k))}">${esc(filterLabel(k))}${ICON.close}</button>`)
    .join("");
}

function renderFilterSheet() {
  setDialogHTML(filterSheet, `
    <header class="sheet-head">
      <div class="sheet-title"><h2 id="filter-title" tabindex="-1">Filters</h2></div>
      <button type="button" class="sheet-close" data-action="close-filters" aria-label="Close">${ICON.close}</button>
    </header>
    <div class="form">
      ${filterGroups()
        .map(
          (g) => `<fieldset class="field">
            <legend>${esc(g.legend)}</legend>
            <div class="choice-row">${g.options.map(([value, label]) => `<label class="choice"><input type="radio" name="f-${g.key}" value="${value}"${app.filters[g.key] === value ? " checked" : ""}><span>${esc(label)}</span></label>`).join("")}</div>
          </fieldset>`,
        )
        .join("")}
    </div>
    <footer class="form-foot">
      <div class="form-actions">
        <button type="button" class="btn btn-quiet" data-action="reset-filters">Clear filters</button>
        <button type="button" class="btn btn-primary" data-action="close-filters" id="filter-done"></button>
      </div>
    </footer>`);
  updateFilterDone();
}

function updateFilterDone() {
  const done = $("#filter-done", filterSheet);
  if (done) done.textContent = `Show ${plural(foodList.filter((food) => matchesFilters(food)).length, "food")}`;
}

function openFilters() {
  renderFilterSheet();
  filterSheet.showModal();
  $("#filter-title", filterSheet).focus();
}

// Compact tile: icon, name, and small badges that flow after the name. The
// age only shows when a food isn't ready yet.
function tileHTML(food) {
  const s = app.summaries.get(food.id);
  const status = s ? s.status : "new";
  const waiting = isWaiting(food);
  const tags = [];
  if (food.age == null) tags.push('<span class="tag tag-bad" title="Avoid for young children">Avoid</span>');
  else if (waiting) tags.push(`<span class="tag tag-wait" title="${esc(L.formatAgeRule(food))}">${ageTag(food)}</span>`);
  if (food.choking === "high") tags.push(`<span class="tag tag-icon" title="Common choking hazard: prepare carefully">${ICON.hazard}</span>`);
  if (food.iron) tags.push('<span class="tag" title="Iron-rich">Fe</span>');
  if (food.allergens.length) {
    const more = food.allergens.length > 1 ? ` +${food.allergens.length - 1}` : "";
    tags.push(`<span class="tag tag-solid" title="Common allergens: ${esc(food.allergens.map((a) => ALLERGEN_LABEL[a]).join(", "))}">${esc(ALLERGEN_SHORT[food.allergens[0]])}${more}</span>`);
  }
  if (app.state.queue[food.id]) tags.push(`<span class="tag tag-icon" title="On your to-try list">${ICON.bookmark}</span>`);
  const markLabel = s
    ? `${food.name}: tried ${plural(s.count, "time")}, ${STATUS_LABEL[status].toLowerCase()}. Log another try`
    : `Log a first try of ${food.name}`;
  const ageTitle = food.age == null ? "Not recommended for young children" : `${L.formatAgeRule(food)}${waiting ? " (not yet for your baby)" : ""}`;
  const extras = [food.choking === "high" && "Common choking hazard", food.iron && "Iron-rich", food.allergens.length && `Contains ${food.allergens.map((a) => ALLERGEN_LABEL[a].toLowerCase()).join(", ")}`].filter(Boolean);
  const mark = MARK[status] + (s && s.count > 1 ? `<span class="tile-count">${s.count}</span>` : "");
  return `<div class="tile st-${status}" data-cat="${food.cat}">
    <button type="button" class="tile-open" data-open="${food.id}" aria-label="${esc([food.name, ageTitle, ...extras, STATUS_LABEL[status]].join(". "))}.">
      ${iconHTML(food, "tile-icon")}
      <span class="tile-name">${esc(food.name)}${tags.length ? `<span class="tile-tags" aria-hidden="true">${tags.join("")}</span>` : ""}</span>
    </button>
    ${canWrite() ? `<button type="button" class="tile-check${app.popFood === food.id ? " pop" : ""}" data-action="log" data-food="${food.id}" aria-label="${esc(markLabel)}">${mark}</button>` : `<span class="tile-check" aria-hidden="true">${mark}</span>`}
  </div>`;
}

function legendHTML() {
  if (!app.summaries.size) return "";
  return `<span class="legend" aria-hidden="true">${Object.entries(STATUS_SHORT)
    .map(([status, label]) => `<span class="st-${status}">${MARK[status]}${label}</span>`)
    .join("")}</span>`;
}

function sortFoods(list) {
  if (app.filters.sort === "az") return [...list].sort((a, b) => a.name.localeCompare(b.name));
  if (app.filters.sort === "recent") {
    return [...list].sort((a, b) => {
      const da = app.summaries.get(a.id)?.lastDate || "";
      const db = app.summaries.get(b.id)?.lastDate || "";
      if (da !== db) return da < db ? 1 : -1;
      return a.name.localeCompare(b.name);
    });
  }
  return list;
}

function renderFoodResults() {
  const box = $("#food-results");
  if (!box) return;
  renderStatusChips();
  const list = sortFoods(foodList.filter((food) => matchesFilters(food)));
  const tried = list.filter((food) => app.summaries.has(food.id)).length;
  renderFilterBar();
  main.classList.toggle("is-loading", loading());
  const line = `<p class="result-line"><span class="num">${plural(list.length, "food")} · ${tried} tried</span>${loading() ? '<span class="skeleton skeleton-line" role="status"><span class="visually-hidden">Loading your tracker…</span></span>' : legendHTML()}</p>`;
  const groups = app.filters.sort === "category" ? CATEGORIES.map((c) => ({ ...c, foods: list.filter((food) => food.cat === c.id) })).filter((g) => g.foods.length) : [];
  renderCatJump(groups);
  requestAnimationFrame(updateScrollUI);
  // Searching for something that isn't listed: offer to add it.
  const q = app.filters.q.trim();
  const addOwn = q && canWrite() ? `<button type="button" class="btn btn-small${list.length ? "" : " btn-primary"}" data-action="add-food" data-name="${esc(q)}">${ICON.plus}Add “${esc(q)}” as your own food</button>` : "";
  if (!list.length) {
    box.innerHTML = `${line}<div class="empty"><strong>No foods match</strong>Try a different search, or clear the filters.<div class="button-row empty-actions">${addOwn}<button type="button" class="btn btn-small" data-action="clear-filters">Clear filters</button></div></div>`;
    return;
  }
  const after = addOwn ? `<p class="add-own">Not the one you meant? ${addOwn}</p>` : "";
  if (!groups.length) {
    box.innerHTML = `${line}<div class="grid">${list.map(tileHTML).join("")}</div>${after}`;
    return;
  }
  box.innerHTML =
    line +
    groups
      .map((g) => {
        const gTried = g.foods.filter((food) => app.summaries.has(food.id)).length;
        return `<section class="group" data-cat="${g.id}" aria-labelledby="group-${g.id}">
          <div class="group-head"><h2 id="group-${g.id}">${esc(g.label)}</h2><span class="num">${gTried}/${g.foods.length} tried</span></div>
          <div class="grid">${g.foods.map(tileHTML).join("")}</div>
        </section>`;
      })
      .join("") +
    after;
}

// ---------- food sheet ----------

function factsHTML(food) {
  const facts = [];
  if (food.age == null) facts.push('<span class="fact fact-bad">Avoid for young children</span>');
  else facts.push(`<span class="fact${isWaiting(food) ? " fact-warn" : " fact-good"}">${esc(L.formatAgeRule(food))}</span>`);
  // A family food without listed ingredients has no known choking risk.
  if (food.choking) {
    const choke = CHOKING[food.choking];
    facts.push(`<span class="fact ${food.choking === "high" ? "fact-bad" : food.choking === "prep" ? "fact-warn" : ""}">${esc(choke.label)}</span>`);
  }
  if (food.iron) facts.push('<span class="fact fact-iron">Iron-rich</span>');
  for (const a of food.allergens) facts.push(`<span class="fact fact-warn">Allergen: ${esc(ALLERGEN_LABEL[a])}</span>`);
  for (const a of food.mayContain || []) facts.push(`<span class="fact">May contain: ${esc(ALLERGEN_LABEL[a].toLowerCase())}</span>`);
  return `<div class="facts">${facts.join("")}</div>`;
}

// One line near the top of a food's page: how it's going so far.
function statusLineHTML(food) {
  const s = app.summaries.get(food.id);
  if (!s) return loading() ? '<span class="skeleton skeleton-line" role="status"><span class="visually-hidden">Loading your log…</span></span>' : loadError() ? "<p>Your log didn't load.</p>" : "";
  const pastReaction = s.status !== "reaction" && s.lastReactionDate;
  // Reactions after a meal this food was in: a caution, not a verdict.
  const mealReactions = (s.mealReactions || [])
    .filter((r) => foodById(r.mealId))
    .map((r) => `<span class="reaction-note">Reaction noted on ${esc(fmtDate(r.date))} after ${esc(foodById(r.mealId).name)}, which has ${esc(food.name.toLowerCase())} in it</span>`);
  return `<span class="status-pill st-${s.status}${app.popFood === food.id ? " pop" : ""}">${MARK[s.status]}${esc(STATUS_LABEL[s.status])}</span>
    <span>Tried ${plural(s.count, "time")}${s.mealCount ? ` (${s.mealCount} in meals)` : ""}, last on ${esc(fmtDate(s.lastDate))}</span>
    ${pastReaction ? `<span class="reaction-note">Reaction noted on ${esc(fmtDate(s.lastReactionDate))}</span>` : ""}
    ${mealReactions.join("")}`;
}

// The family's own foods that have this one in them.
function mealsWith(food) {
  return foodList.filter((f) => f.custom && f.ingredients.includes(food.id));
}

function historyHTML(food) {
  const logs = L.logsForFood(app.state, food.id).reverse();
  const meals = mealsWith(food);
  const inMeals = meals.length
    ? `<p class="in-meals">Also in your foods: ${meals.map((m) => `<button type="button" class="chip chip-link" data-open="${m.id}">${esc(m.name)}</button>`).join("")}</p>`
    : "";
  if (!logs.length) return inMeals ? `<h3>Our tries</h3>${inMeals}` : "";
  return `<h3>Our tries</h3>${inMeals}<ul class="history">${logs
    .map((log) => {
      const who = whoLogged(log);
      const details = [log.served && `Served: ${esc(log.served)}`, log.notes && esc(log.notes), who && `Logged by ${esc(who)}`].filter(Boolean);
      const reactions = (log.reactions || []).map((r) => REACTION_LABEL[r] || r);
      return `<li>
        <span class="when">${esc(fmtDate(log.date))} · ${esc(OUTCOME_LABEL[log.outcome])}</span>
        ${canWrite() ? `<button type="button" class="btn btn-small btn-quiet" data-action="edit-log" data-food="${food.id}" data-log="${esc(log.id)}">Edit</button>` : "<span></span>"}
        ${reactions.length ? `<p class="detail reaction-line">Reaction: ${esc(reactions.join(", "))}</p>` : ""}
        ${details.length ? `<p class="detail">${details.join(" · ")}</p>` : ""}
      </li>`;
    })
    .join("")}</ul>`;
}

// Pinned to the bottom of a food's page, so logging is always one tap away.
function sheetFootHTML(food) {
  if (!canWrite()) return "";
  const queued = !!app.state.queue[food.id];
  // Foods to avoid don't belong on a to-try list (unless already there).
  const queueButton =
    food.age != null || queued
      ? `<button type="button" class="btn" id="sheet-queue" data-action="queue" data-food="${food.id}" aria-pressed="${queued}">${ICON.bookmark}<span>${queued ? "On to-try list" : "To-try list"}</span></button>`
      : "";
  return `<footer class="sheet-foot">
    <button type="button" class="btn btn-primary" data-action="log" data-food="${food.id}">${ICON.plus}<span id="sheet-log-label">${app.summaries.has(food.id) ? "Log another try" : "Log a try"}</span></button>
    ${queueButton}
  </footer>`;
}

// Redraw the parts of an open food page that depend on the log.
function refreshSheetLog(food) {
  const status = $("#sheet-status", sheet);
  status.innerHTML = statusLineHTML(food);
  status.hidden = !status.innerHTML.trim();
  const history = $("#sheet-log", sheet);
  history.innerHTML = historyHTML(food);
  history.hidden = !history.innerHTML;
  // A page opened before the log loaded has no log bar yet.
  const foot = $(".sheet-foot", sheet);
  if (!foot && canWrite()) sheet.insertAdjacentHTML("beforeend", sheetFootHTML(food));
  else if (foot && !canWrite()) foot.remove();
  const label = $("#sheet-log-label", sheet);
  if (label) label.textContent = app.summaries.has(food.id) ? "Log another try" : "Log a try";
  const queue = $("#sheet-queue", sheet);
  if (queue) {
    const queued = !!app.state.queue[food.id];
    queue.setAttribute("aria-pressed", String(queued));
    $("span", queue).textContent = queued ? "On to-try list" : "To-try list";
  }
}

function serveHTML(food) {
  if (food.age == null) return "";
  const months = babyMonths();
  const nowStage = L.stageForAge(months);
  const stage = app.stage || nowStage;
  const tabs = STAGES.map(
    (s) =>
      `<button type="button" role="tab" class="stage-btn" data-stage="${s.id}" aria-selected="${s.id === stage}" aria-controls="serve-panel">${esc(s.short)}${months != null && months >= 6 && s.id === nowStage ? '<span class="now">Now</span>' : ""}</button>`,
  ).join("");
  const st = STAGES.find((s) => s.id === stage);
  const next = STAGES[STAGES.indexOf(st) + 1];
  const tooEarly = food.age >= (next ? next.from : Infinity);
  let text = food.serve[stage];
  if (tooEarly || !text) text = `<strong>Not yet.</strong> ${esc(L.formatAgeRule(food))}.`;
  else text = esc(text);
  return `<section>
    <h3>How to serve it</h3>
    <div class="stages" role="tablist" aria-label="Age">${tabs}</div>
    <p class="serve-text" id="serve-panel" role="tabpanel">${text}</p>
  </section>`;
}

// What's in a family food, its label and notes, and buttons to change it.
function customFoodHTML(food) {
  const parts = food.ingredients.map((id) => foodById(id)).filter(Boolean);
  const inside = parts.length
    ? `<section><h3>What's in it</h3>
        <div class="ingredient-list">${parts.map((f) => `<button type="button" class="ingredient" data-open="${f.id}"><span class="mini-tile" data-cat="${f.cat}" aria-hidden="true">${iconHTML(f)}</span>${esc(f.name)}</button>`).join("")}</div>
        <p class="muted">Each try of ${esc(food.name)} also counts as a try of these. Open one for how to serve it safely.</p>
      </section>`
    : `<section><h3>How to serve it</h3><p>There's no serving guide for foods you add. See <a href="#guide" data-jump-guide="sizes">Sizes and shapes by age</a> in the Guide, or ask your pediatrician.</p></section>`;
  return `${inside}
    ${food.label ? `<section><h3>Ingredients on the label</h3><p class="label-text">${esc(food.label)}</p></section>` : ""}
    ${food.notes ? `<section><h3>Notes</h3><p class="label-text">${esc(food.notes)}</p></section>` : ""}
    ${canWrite() ? `<div class="button-row">
      <button type="button" class="btn btn-small" data-action="edit-food" data-food="${food.id}">Edit food</button>
      <button type="button" class="btn btn-small btn-danger" data-action="delete-food" data-food="${food.id}">Delete food</button>
    </div>` : ""}`;
}

function renderSheet() {
  const food = foodById(app.openFoodId);
  if (!food) return;
  app.sheetSource = food.source || null;
  const ssUrl = food.ss ? `https://solidstarts.com/foods/${encodeURIComponent(food.ss)}/` : null;
  const searchUrl = `https://www.google.com/search?q=${encodeURIComponent(`site:solidstarts.com ${food.name} for babies`)}`;
  setDialogHTML(sheet, `
    <header class="sheet-head">
      <div class="big-tile" data-cat="${food.cat}" aria-hidden="true">${iconHTML(food)}</div>
      <div class="sheet-title">
        <p class="eyebrow">${esc(CAT_LABEL[food.cat])}${food.custom ? " · Your food" : ""}</p>
        <h2 id="sheet-title" tabindex="-1">${esc(food.name)}</h2>
        ${food.custom ? (food.brand ? `<p class="muted">${esc(food.brand)}</p>` : "") : food.aka.length ? `<p class="muted">Also: ${esc(food.aka.join(", "))}</p>` : ""}
      </div>
      <button type="button" class="sheet-close" data-action="close-sheet" aria-label="Close">${ICON.close}</button>
    </header>
    <div class="sheet-body">
      ${factsHTML(food)}
      ${food.wait ? `<div class="wait-box${food.age == null ? " is-avoid" : ""}"><strong>${food.age == null ? "Avoid." : `Wait until ${esc(L.formatAgeRule(food).replace("From ", ""))}.`}</strong> ${esc(food.wait)}</div>` : ""}
      <div class="log-status" id="sheet-status" aria-label="Our log"></div>
      ${food.custom ? customFoodHTML(food) : serveHTML(food)}
      ${food.tips.length ? `<section><h3>Good to know</h3><ul class="tips">${food.tips.map((t) => `<li>${esc(t)}</li>`).join("")}</ul></section>` : ""}
      <section id="sheet-log" aria-label="Our tries"></section>
      ${food.custom ? "" : `<div class="source-links">
        ${ssUrl ? `<a href="${ssUrl}" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Full guide on Solid Starts ↗</a>` : `<a href="${searchUrl}" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Search Solid Starts for ${esc(food.name.toLowerCase())} ↗</a>`}
      </div>`}
      <p class="disclaimer">General guidance only. Every baby is different; check with your pediatrician, especially about allergies.</p>
    </div>
    ${sheetFootHTML(food)}`);
  refreshSheetLog(food);
}

function showSheet(id) {
  if (app.openFoodId !== id) app.stage = null;
  app.openFoodId = id;
  renderSheet();
  if (!sheet.open) sheet.showModal();
  sheet.scrollTop = 0;
  $("#sheet-title", sheet).focus();
}

function hideSheet() {
  app.openFoodId = null;
  if (sheet.open) sheet.close();
}

function openFood(id) {
  app.pushedFood = true;
  location.hash = `food-${id}`;
}

function closeFood() {
  if (app.pushedFood && location.hash.startsWith("#food-")) {
    app.pushedFood = false;
    history.back();
  } else {
    location.replace(`#${app.view}`);
  }
}

// ---------- adding the family's own foods ----------

// While the form is open: the food's id (when editing), what's in it, and
// the allergens ticked by hand.
let ff = null;

function capitalize(text) {
  return text ? text[0].toUpperCase() + text.slice(1) : text;
}

function openFoodForm({ id = null, name = "" } = {}) {
  if (!canWrite()) return;
  const existing = id ? app.state.foods[id] : null;
  ff = {
    id: existing ? existing.id : null,
    ingredients: existing ? [...existing.ingredients] : [],
    allergens: new Set(existing ? existing.allergens : []),
    mayContain: new Set(existing ? existing.mayContain : []),
  };
  const cat = existing ? existing.cat : "mixed";
  setDialogHTML(foodForm, `
    <form class="log-form" id="food-form-el" novalidate>
      <header class="sheet-head">
        <div class="big-tile big-tile-small" id="ff-tile" data-cat="${cat}" aria-hidden="true"></div>
        <div class="sheet-title"><p class="eyebrow">${existing ? "Edit your food" : "Add a food"}</p><h2 id="ff-title" tabindex="-1">${existing ? esc(existing.name) : "New food"}</h2></div>
        <button type="button" class="sheet-close" data-action="close-food-form" aria-label="Close">${ICON.close}</button>
      </header>
      <div class="form">
        <div id="ff-reader">${labelReaderHTML()}</div>
        <div class="field">
          <label for="ff-name">Name</label>
          <input class="input" id="ff-name" name="foodname" maxlength="60" autocomplete="off" required value="${esc(existing ? existing.name : capitalize(name))}" placeholder="e.g. Potato & beef purée, kangaroo">
        </div>
        <fieldset class="field">
          <legend>Category</legend>
          <div class="choice-row">${CATEGORIES.map((c) => `<label class="choice"><input type="radio" name="cat" value="${c.id}"${c.id === cat ? " checked" : ""}><span>${esc(c.label)}</span></label>`).join("")}</div>
        </fieldset>
        <fieldset class="field">
          <legend>What's in it? <span class="muted">(optional)</span></legend>
          <p class="hint">Pick foods from the list. Logging this then counts as a try of each, and their age, choking and allergen advice carries over.</p>
          <div class="choice-row" id="ff-picked"></div>
          <div class="search search-small">
            ${ICON.search}
            <label class="visually-hidden" for="ff-ing">Find an ingredient</label>
            <input id="ff-ing" type="search" autocomplete="off" enterkeyhint="search" placeholder="Add an ingredient, e.g. potato">
          </div>
          <div class="choice-row" id="ff-ing-results"></div>
        </fieldset>
        <fieldset class="field">
          <legend>Allergens</legend>
          <p class="hint">Ingredients you picked tick their own. Add any others the label lists.</p>
          <div class="choice-row" id="ff-allergens"></div>
          <details class="ff-may"${ff.mayContain.size ? " open" : ""}>
            <summary>The label says “may contain”…</summary>
            <div class="choice-row" id="ff-may"></div>
          </details>
        </fieldset>
        <div class="field">
          <label for="ff-brand">Brand <span class="muted">(optional)</span></label>
          <input class="input" id="ff-brand" name="brand" maxlength="60" autocomplete="off" value="${esc(existing ? existing.brand : "")}">
        </div>
        <div class="field">
          <label for="ff-label">Ingredients on the label <span class="muted">(optional)</span></label>
          <textarea class="input" id="ff-label" name="label" maxlength="2000" placeholder="e.g. Potato (60%), beef (25%), water, onion">${esc(existing ? existing.label : "")}</textarea>
        </div>
        <div class="field">
          <label for="ff-notes">Notes <span class="muted">(optional)</span></label>
          <textarea class="input" id="ff-notes" name="notes" maxlength="1000" placeholder="How you serve it, where you buy it…">${esc(existing ? existing.notes : "")}</textarea>
        </div>
      </div>
      <footer class="form-foot">
        <p class="alert" id="ff-error" role="alert" hidden></p>
        <div class="form-actions" id="ff-actions">
          <button type="button" class="btn btn-quiet" data-action="close-food-form">Cancel</button>
          <button type="submit" class="btn btn-primary">${existing ? "Save changes" : "Add food"}</button>
        </div>
        <div class="discard-bar" id="ff-discard" hidden>
          <p><strong>Discard ${existing ? "your changes" : "this food"}?</strong> What you've entered won't be saved.</p>
          <div class="form-actions">
            <button type="button" class="btn btn-quiet" data-action="ff-keep">Keep editing</button>
            <button type="button" class="btn btn-danger" data-action="ff-discard">Discard</button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </footer>
    </form>`);
  updateFoodFormParts();
  foodForm.dataset.initial = foodFormSnapshot();
  if (!foodForm.open) foodForm.showModal();
  $(existing || name ? "#ff-title" : "#ff-name", foodForm).focus();
}

// Reading a label needs Firebase (it goes through your own project) and the
// reCAPTCHA key that switches it on.
function labelReaderHTML() {
  if (!app.store || app.store.mode !== "cloud") return "";
  if (!app.labelSettings) {
    return `<p class="form-note">${ICON.camera}Reading labels from a photo isn't switched on yet. See “Reading food labels from a photo” in the README.</p>`;
  }
  return `<div class="label-reader">
    <label class="btn btn-primary" for="ff-photo" id="ff-photo-btn">${ICON.camera}Read a label from a photo</label>
    <input type="file" id="ff-photo" accept="image/*" capture="environment" class="visually-hidden">
    <p class="hint">Photograph the ingredients and allergen statement. Google's Gemini reads the photo and fills in the form for you to check. Protected by reCAPTCHA: Google's <a href="https://policies.google.com/privacy" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Privacy Policy</a> and <a href="https://policies.google.com/terms" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Terms</a> apply.</p>
    <div id="ff-read-status" role="status"></div>
  </div>`;
}

// Shrink a photo to a JPEG the model can read quickly (about 1600px on the
// long side), as base64.
async function photoToJPEG(file, longest = 1600) {
  const bitmap = await createImageBitmap(file);
  const scale = Math.min(1, longest / Math.max(bitmap.width, bitmap.height));
  const canvas = document.createElement("canvas");
  canvas.width = Math.round(bitmap.width * scale);
  canvas.height = Math.round(bitmap.height * scale);
  canvas.getContext("2d").drawImage(bitmap, 0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);
  const blob = await new Promise((resolve) => canvas.toBlob(resolve, "image/jpeg", 0.85));
  const bytes = new Uint8Array(await blob.arrayBuffer());
  let binary = "";
  for (let i = 0; i < bytes.length; i += 0x8000) binary += String.fromCharCode(...bytes.subarray(i, i + 0x8000));
  return btoa(binary);
}

async function readLabelPhoto(file) {
  const status = $("#ff-read-status", foodForm);
  const button = $("#ff-photo-btn", foodForm);
  status.innerHTML = '<p class="reading"><span class="spinner" aria-hidden="true"></span>Reading the label…</p>';
  button.classList.add("is-busy");
  try {
    const base64 = await photoToJPEG(file);
    const answer = await readLabelImage(app.cloudApi, app.labelSettings, { prompt: LABEL_PROMPT, schema: labelSchema, model: LABEL_MODEL }, base64);
    const read = parseLabel(answer, FOODS);
    if (!read.readable) throw Object.assign(new Error("No label in the photo"), { code: "unreadable" });
    if (!foodForm.open || !ff) return;
    applyLabel(read);
    const notes = [];
    if (read.spotted.length) notes.push(`Also ticked ${read.spotted.map((a) => ALLERGEN_LABEL[a].toLowerCase()).join(", ")}, from words in the ingredients.`);
    if (read.unmatched.length) notes.push(`Not in the food list, so not counted: ${read.unmatched.join(", ")}.`);
    status.innerHTML = `<div class="form-note read-ok"><p><strong>Filled in from the label.</strong> Check everything against the pack before saving.</p>${notes.map((n) => `<p>${esc(n)}</p>`).join("")}</div>`;
  } catch (err) {
    console.error(err);
    status.innerHTML = `<p class="alert">${esc(describeLabelError(err))}</p>`;
  } finally {
    button.classList.remove("is-busy");
  }
}

// Fill the form from what was read, keeping anything already typed in.
function applyLabel(read) {
  const form = $("#food-form-el", foodForm);
  if (read.name && !form.foodname.value.trim()) form.foodname.value = read.name;
  if (read.brand && !form.brand.value.trim()) form.brand.value = read.brand;
  if (read.category) form.cat.value = read.category;
  if (read.ingredientsText) form.label.value = read.ingredientsText;
  for (const id of read.ingredientIds) if (!ff.ingredients.includes(id)) ff.ingredients.push(id);
  for (const a of read.contains) ff.allergens.add(a);
  for (const a of read.mayContain) ff.mayContain.add(a);
  if (read.otherAllergens.length) {
    const extra = `The label also lists: ${read.otherAllergens.join(", ")}.`;
    if (!form.notes.value.includes(extra)) form.notes.value = [form.notes.value.trim(), extra].filter(Boolean).join("\n");
  }
  if (read.mayContain.length) $(".ff-may", form).open = true;
  $("#ff-title", foodForm).textContent = form.foodname.value.trim() || "New food";
  updateFoodFormParts();
}

// Allergens the picked ingredients bring, with the first food that brings each.
function ingredientAllergens() {
  const from = new Map();
  for (const id of ff.ingredients) for (const a of FOODS_BY_ID.get(id).allergens) if (!from.has(a)) from.set(a, FOODS_BY_ID.get(id).name);
  return from;
}

function updateFoodFormParts() {
  const form = $("#food-form-el", foodForm);
  if (!form) return;
  const cat = form.cat.value || "mixed";
  const parts = ff.ingredients.map((id) => FOODS_BY_ID.get(id));
  $("#ff-picked", form).innerHTML = parts
    .map((f) => `<button type="button" class="chip chip-on" data-action="ff-remove" data-id="${f.id}" aria-label="Remove ${esc(f.name)}">${esc(f.name)}${ICON.close}</button>`)
    .join("");
  const from = ingredientAllergens();
  $("#ff-allergens", form).innerHTML = ALLERGENS.map((a) => {
    const auto = from.get(a.id);
    return `<button type="button" class="chip" data-action="ff-allergen" data-allergen="${a.id}" aria-pressed="${!!auto || ff.allergens.has(a.id)}"${auto ? " disabled" : ""}>${esc(a.label)}${auto ? ` <span class="count">from ${esc(auto.toLowerCase())}</span>` : ""}</button>`;
  }).join("");
  $("#ff-may", form).innerHTML = ALLERGENS.map((a) => `<button type="button" class="chip" data-action="ff-may" data-allergen="${a.id}" aria-pressed="${ff.mayContain.has(a.id)}">${esc(a.label)}</button>`).join("");
  const preview = L.expandCustomFood({ id: "my-preview", name: "", cat, ingredients: ff.ingredients, allergens: [], mayContain: [], brand: "", notes: "", label: "" }, FOODS_BY_ID, CATEGORY_ICONS);
  const tile = $("#ff-tile", form);
  tile.dataset.cat = cat;
  tile.innerHTML = iconHTML(preview);
  renderIngredientResults();
}

function renderIngredientResults() {
  const box = $("#ff-ing-results", foodForm);
  const q = $("#ff-ing", foodForm).value;
  const nq = L.normalizeText(q);
  if (!nq) {
    box.innerHTML = "";
    return;
  }
  const starts = (f) => (L.normalizeText(f.name).startsWith(nq) ? 0 : 1);
  const matches = FOODS.filter((f) => !ff.ingredients.includes(f.id) && L.matchesQuery(f, q))
    .sort((a, b) => starts(a) - starts(b) || a.name.localeCompare(b.name))
    .slice(0, 8);
  box.innerHTML = matches.length
    ? matches.map((f) => `<button type="button" class="chip" data-action="ff-add" data-id="${f.id}">${ICON.plus}${esc(f.name)}</button>`).join("")
    : `<p class="hint">Nothing in the food list matches. That's fine: just leave it out.</p>`;
}

function foodFormSnapshot() {
  const form = $("#food-form-el", foodForm);
  if (!form || !ff) return "";
  return JSON.stringify([form.foodname.value, form.cat.value, form.brand.value, form.label.value, form.notes.value, ff.ingredients, [...ff.allergens].sort(), [...ff.mayContain].sort()]);
}

function setFoodFormDiscard(show) {
  $("#ff-discard", foodForm).hidden = !show;
  $("#ff-actions", foodForm).hidden = show;
  if (show) $("#ff-error", foodForm).hidden = true;
  $(show ? '[data-action="ff-keep"]' : '[type="submit"]', foodForm).focus();
}

function requestCloseFoodForm() {
  if (!foodForm.open) return;
  if (foodFormSnapshot() === foodForm.dataset.initial) foodForm.close();
  else setFoodFormDiscard(true);
}

function showFoodFormError(message, field) {
  const el = $("#ff-error", foodForm);
  el.textContent = message;
  el.hidden = false;
  if (field) {
    field.scrollIntoView({ block: "center", behavior: smooth() });
    field.focus({ preventScroll: true });
  }
}

async function submitFoodForm(form) {
  // (form.name is the form's own name, so the field is called foodname.)
  const name = form.foodname.value.trim();
  if (!name) return showFoodFormError("Give the food a name.", form.foodname);
  const clash = FOODS.find((f) => L.normalizeText(f.name) === L.normalizeText(name));
  if (clash) return showFoodFormError(`${clash.name} is already in the food list. Search for it on the Foods tab.`, form.foodname);
  const existing = ff.id ? app.state.foods[ff.id] : null;
  const now = Date.now();
  // Only allergens ticked by hand are kept: the ingredients' own are worked
  // out each time, so they stay right if the ingredients change.
  const from = ingredientAllergens();
  const food = L.cleanCustomFood(
    {
      ...(existing || {}),
      id: existing ? existing.id : L.newCustomId(name),
      name,
      cat: form.cat.value,
      ingredients: ff.ingredients,
      allergens: [...ff.allergens].filter((a) => !from.has(a)),
      mayContain: [...ff.mayContain],
      brand: form.brand.value,
      label: form.label.value,
      notes: form.notes.value,
      createdAt: existing ? existing.createdAt : now,
      updatedAt: now,
    },
    KNOWN_IDS,
  );
  const submit = form.querySelector('[type="submit"]');
  submit.disabled = true;
  try {
    await app.store.saveFood(food);
    foodForm.close();
    if (existing) {
      toast("Saved changes", { undo: () => app.store.saveFood(existing) });
    } else {
      toast(`Added ${food.name}`, { undo: () => app.store.deleteFood(food.id) });
      openFood(food.id);
    }
  } catch (err) {
    showFoodFormError(describeStoreError(err));
  } finally {
    submit.disabled = false;
  }
}

// Deleting a family food takes its tries with it; Undo puts them all back.
async function deleteFood(id) {
  const food = app.state.foods[id];
  if (!food) return;
  const logs = Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(app.state.logs).filter(([, l]) => l.foodId === id));
  const queued = app.state.queue[id];
  closeFood();
  try {
    await app.store.deleteFood(id);
    const n = Object.keys(logs).length;
    toast(`Deleted ${food.name}${n ? ` and its ${plural(n, "try", "tries")}` : ""}`, {
      undo: () => app.store.importBackup({ app: "first-bites", foods: { [id]: food }, logs, queue: queued ? { [id]: queued } : {} }),
    });
  } catch (err) {
    toast(describeStoreError(err));
  }
}

// ---------- log dialog ----------

// A heads-up when logging a food that isn't recommended yet at this age.
function logWarningHTML(food) {
  if (food.age != null && !isWaiting(food)) return "";
  const reason = food.wait ? ` ${esc(food.wait)}` : "";
  if (food.age == null) return `<div class="wait-box is-avoid"><strong>${esc(food.name)} is best avoided for young children.</strong>${reason}</div>`;
  const months = babyMonths();
  const age = months != null ? ` ${esc(babyName() || "Your baby")} is ${esc(L.formatAgeLong(months))}.` : "";
  return `<div class="wait-box"><strong>${esc(food.name)} isn't recommended until ${esc(L.formatAgeRule(food).replace("From ", ""))}.</strong>${age}${reason}</div>`;
}

// What the form holds now, to tell whether closing it would lose anything.
function logFormSnapshot() {
  const form = $("#log-form", logDialog);
  if (!form) return "";
  return JSON.stringify([...new FormData(form).entries(), $("#log-has-reaction", form).checked]);
}

function setLogDiscardBar(show) {
  $("#log-discard", logDialog).hidden = !show;
  $("#log-actions", logDialog).hidden = show;
  if (show) $("#log-error", logDialog).hidden = true;
  $(show ? '[data-action="keep-editing"]' : '[type="submit"]', logDialog).focus();
}

// Close the form, but ask first if that would throw away what was entered.
function requestCloseLog() {
  if (!logDialog.open) return;
  if (logFormSnapshot() === logDialog.dataset.initial) logDialog.close();
  else setLogDiscardBar(true);
}

function phoneOnlyNoteHTML() {
  if (!phoneOnly() || app.cloud.status === "loading") return "";
  return `<p class="form-note">${app.cloud.status === "signed-out" ? "You're signed out" : "Sync isn't connected"}, so this try will be saved on this phone only.</p>`;
}

// The quick way to log: one tap on a face saves today's try. Details open
// the full form in the same dialog.
function openQuickLog(foodId) {
  if (!canWrite()) return;
  const food = foodById(foodId);
  setDialogHTML(logDialog, `
    <header class="sheet-head">
      <div class="big-tile big-tile-small" data-cat="${food.cat}" aria-hidden="true">${iconHTML(food)}</div>
      <div class="sheet-title"><p class="eyebrow">Log a try</p><h2 id="log-title" tabindex="-1">${esc(food.name)}</h2></div>
      <button type="button" class="sheet-close" data-action="close-log" aria-label="Close">${ICON.close}</button>
    </header>
    <div class="form">
      ${logWarningHTML(food)}
      ${phoneOnlyNoteHTML()}
      <fieldset class="field">
        <legend>How did it go today?</legend>
        <div class="face-row">
          ${OUTCOMES.map((o) => `<button type="button" class="face-btn" data-action="quick-save" data-outcome="${o.id}"><svg class="face-icon" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use href="#ic-${o.icon}"></use></svg><span>${esc(o.label)}</span></button>`).join("")}
        </div>
      </fieldset>
      <div class="quick-more">
        <button type="button" class="btn btn-small" data-action="log-details">Add date, notes or how it was served</button>
        <button type="button" class="btn btn-small btn-danger" data-action="log-reaction">Noticed a reaction?</button>
      </div>
    </div>`);
  logDialog.dataset.food = foodId;
  logDialog.dataset.log = "";
  logDialog.dataset.initial = logFormSnapshot();
  if (!logDialog.open) logDialog.showModal();
  $("#log-title", logDialog).focus();
}

async function quickSave(button) {
  const foodId = logDialog.dataset.food;
  const outcome = button.dataset.outcome;
  const now = Date.now();
  for (const b of logDialog.querySelectorAll(".face-btn")) b.disabled = true;
  const id = L.newId();
  markLogged(foodId);
  try {
    await app.store.saveLog({ id, foodId, date: L.todayISO(), outcome, reactions: [], served: "", notes: "", createdAt: now, updatedAt: now });
    logDialog.close();
    toast(`Saved: ${foodById(foodId).name}, ${OUTCOME_LABEL[outcome].toLowerCase()}`, { undo: () => app.store.deleteLog(id) });
  } catch (err) {
    for (const b of logDialog.querySelectorAll(".face-btn")) b.disabled = false;
    toast(describeStoreError(err));
  }
}

function openLogDialog(foodId, logId, { reaction = false } = {}) {
  if (!canWrite()) return;
  const food = foodById(foodId);
  const existing = logId ? app.state.logs[logId] : null;
  const log = existing || { date: L.todayISO(), outcome: "", reactions: [], served: "", notes: "" };
  const hasReaction = reaction || (log.reactions || []).length > 0;
  const when = log.date === L.todayISO() ? "today" : log.date === L.daysAgoISO(1) ? "yesterday" : "other";
  const served = servedParts(log.served);
  setDialogHTML(logDialog, `
    <form class="log-form" id="log-form" novalidate>
      <header class="sheet-head">
        <div class="big-tile big-tile-small" data-cat="${food.cat}" aria-hidden="true">${iconHTML(food)}</div>
        <div class="sheet-title"><p class="eyebrow">${existing ? "Edit try" : "Log a try"}</p><h2 id="log-title" tabindex="-1">${esc(food.name)}</h2></div>
        <button type="button" class="sheet-close" data-action="close-log" aria-label="Close">${ICON.close}</button>
      </header>
      <div class="form">
        ${existing ? "" : logWarningHTML(food)}
        ${phoneOnlyNoteHTML()}
        <fieldset class="field">
          <legend>When?</legend>
          <div class="choice-row">
            ${[["today", "Today"], ["yesterday", "Yesterday"], ["other", "Another day"]].map(([id, label]) => `<button type="button" class="chip" data-action="log-date" data-when="${id}" aria-pressed="${when === id}">${label}</button>`).join("")}
          </div>
          <label class="visually-hidden" for="log-date">Date of this try</label>
          <input class="input" type="date" id="log-date" name="date" required max="${L.todayISO()}" value="${esc(log.date)}"${when === "other" ? "" : " hidden"}>
        </fieldset>
        <fieldset class="field">
          <legend>How did it go?</legend>
          <div class="choice-row">
            ${OUTCOMES.map((o) => `<label class="choice"><input type="radio" name="outcome" value="${o.id}"${log.outcome === o.id ? " checked" : ""} required><span>${esc(o.label)}</span></label>`).join("")}
          </div>
        </fieldset>
        <div class="field">
          <label for="log-served">How was it served? <span class="muted">(optional)</span></label>
          <div class="choice-row">
            ${SERVED_STYLES.map((style) => `<button type="button" class="chip" data-action="served-pick" data-text="${esc(style.toLowerCase())}" aria-pressed="${served.includes(style.toLowerCase())}">${esc(style)}</button>`).join("")}
          </div>
          <input class="input" id="log-served" name="served" maxlength="300" value="${esc(log.served)}" placeholder="e.g. steamed spears, mashed, on toast">
        </div>
        <fieldset class="field">
          <legend>Any signs of a reaction?</legend>
          <label class="check"><input type="checkbox" id="log-has-reaction"${hasReaction ? " checked" : ""}><span>Yes, we noticed something</span></label>
          <div class="checks" id="reaction-list"${hasReaction ? "" : " hidden"}>
            ${REACTIONS.map((r) => `<label class="check"><input type="checkbox" name="reactions" value="${r.id}"${(log.reactions || []).includes(r.id) ? " checked" : ""}><span>${esc(r.label)}</span></label>`).join("")}
            <p class="alert"><strong>Call emergency services</strong> for trouble breathing, swelling of the tongue or throat, repeated vomiting, or if baby seems pale, floppy or unusually sleepy. For milder symptoms, stop offering the food and call your pediatrician.</p>
          </div>
        </fieldset>
        <div class="field">
          <label for="log-notes">Notes <span class="muted">(optional)</span></label>
          <textarea class="input" id="log-notes" name="notes" maxlength="2000" placeholder="Faces, gagging, how much they ate, what to try next time…">${esc(log.notes)}</textarea>
        </div>
      </div>
      <footer class="form-foot">
        <p class="alert" id="log-error" role="alert" hidden></p>
        <div class="form-actions" id="log-actions">
          ${existing ? `<button type="button" class="btn btn-danger" data-action="delete-log" data-log="${esc(existing.id)}">Delete</button><span class="spacer"></span>` : ""}
          <button type="button" class="btn btn-quiet" data-action="close-log">Cancel</button>
          <button type="submit" class="btn btn-primary">${existing ? "Save changes" : "Save try"}</button>
        </div>
        <div class="discard-bar" id="log-discard" hidden>
